LP solve with FISTA:
```
def lp_solve_fista(a_lp, M, n_iters, beta, x_init=None, x_max=100.0):
    n=len(a); x = init or ones/n; u=x; t=1; phi=inf; tau=1.0
    normA = max(abs(rfft(a_lp, Mfft)))
    base_L = beta*normA*normA + 1e-12
    tau = 1.0 / (base_L * 0.5?) maybe.
    for it:
       c = cross_conv(a_lp,x)
       z=beta*(c-M); p=softmax
       grad = cross_corr? A^T p - 1
       # gradient of φ = A^T p -1 (since derivative of LSE is p; A^T p; -sum -> -1)
       # Want min φ. FISTA: v = u - tau*grad; project box
       # backtracking?
```

Let's design `lse_objective(a, beta)` robust:
Use full c. Want score small. Scale-invariant smooth objective:
```
def lse_score(a, beta):
    S=sum(a)
    if S<eps: return inf
    c=conv_self(a)
    v=c/(S*S)
    z=beta*v
    zmax=z.max()
    return 2*n*(zmax + log(sum(exp(z-zmax)))/beta)
```
This approximates score. Gradient:
- p=exp(z-zmax)/sum
- g=trans_conv(a,p)  # g_i=∑ a_j p_{i+j}
- grad = 2n*(2*g/(S*S) - 2*(c.dot(p))/(S**3))
Check q=∑ p c, p normalized. grad q=2g. grad L = grad(q)/S² - 2q/S³. times 2n. Good.
Scale invariant. We can use L-BFGS-B with bounds (0,1000). But if sum can vary, objective has flat scale; LSE of v = c/S² scale invariant exactly? Let's check scaling: v(λa)=λ²c/λ²S²=v. So objective exactly scale invariant for any β? Yes because logsum of βv. Good. L-BFGS can wander in scale causing ill-conditioning. We can reduce by normalizing to sum=1 after each function eval? Projected simplex:
```
def proj_simplex(v):
    # v length n, enforce sum=1, >=0
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(v)
    rho = np.nonzero(v * np.arange(1,n+1) > cssv - 1)[0][-1]
    theta = (v[:rho+1].sum() -1)/ (rho+1)
    return np.maximum(v - theta,0)
```
But if v sum not 1. Need robust. We can optimize over nonnegative not fixed sum due scale invariance; projection to sum=1 ensures scale. For LSE score with S=1, gradient as above. FFT utilities:
```
def next_pow2(m): return 1 << int(np.ceil(np.log2(m)))
def fft_convolve(a,b):
   n=len(a); m=len(b); L=n+m-1; M=next_pow2(L)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,M); B=np.fft.rfft(b,M)
   return np.fft.irfft(A*B,M)[:L]
```

Let's formulate natural gradient objective on z with LSE full. It can be run with Adam:
```
def natural_grad_step(z, beta, lr, momentum, gradcache):
   a=softmax(z)
   c=conv_self(a)
   maxc=c.max()
   # adaptive beta_target: keep beta*maxc ~ 20? But if beta changes, grad changed.
   p=softmax(beta*c)
   g=trans_conv(a,p)
   grad_a=2*g
   grad_z=a*(grad_a - dot(a,grad_a))
   # return score_LSE, grad_z, c, p, a
```

`softmax_lse_and_grad(z,beta)`:

```
def lp_solve_correct(a, M, beta, n_iters, x0=None, x_max=50.0, verbose=False):
   n=len(a); m=2*n-1
   a = np.maximum(a,0).copy(); S=a.sum(); a=a/S
   # floor: a_lp=(1-floor)*a + floor/n? floor=1e-5 maybe
   M3=next_pow2(3*n-2); Af=rfft(a_lp,M3)
   M2=next_pow2(2*n-1); Af2=rfft(a_lp,M2)
   x = ...
   u=x.copy(); t=1; best_phi=inf; best_x=x
   L=beta*1.1 + 1e-12 # normA^2<=1
   tau=1.0/(L*2.0)
   for it in range(n_iters):
      Xf=rfft(x,M2); c=irfft(Af2*Xf,M2)[:m]
      z=beta*(c-M); zmax=z.max(); expz=np.exp(z-zmax); p=expz/expz.sum()
      phi = -x.sum() + (zmax + log(expz.sum()))/beta
      if phi < best_phi: best_phi=phi; best_x=x.copy()
      # if increase from recent? maybe restart
      P= rfft(p[::-1], M3); h=irfft(Af*P,M3)[:3*n-2]; g=h[m-1:m-n:-1]
      grad = g - 1.0
      # FISTA update
      v = u - tau*grad
      v = np.clip(v,0,x_max)
      t_new=(1+sqrt(1+4*t*t))/2
      u_new = v + (t-1)/t_new*(v-x)
      # accept if phi? no evaluation. To be safe, project u
      x = np.clip(v,0,x_max); u=np.clip(u_new,0,x_max); t=t_new
      # periodic restart if phi increased a lot over last 100
   return best_x
```

def lp_alternate_correct(a, time_limit, beta_list=(100,300,800), max_outer=50, rng=None):
    a = normalize
    best_a=a; best_sc=score
    x_warm=None
    no_improve=0
    ann=0.02
    for beta in beta_list (repeat cycles):
       while time:
          M=conv_self(a).max()/ (sum^2?) normalized so M=maxc
          x=lp_solve_correct(a, M, beta, iters, x_warm, time_left estimate)
          if time break
          normalize x
          # line search current a to x
          a_try, sc_try = mix_line_search(a,x)
          # also try perturb x
          if sc_try < best_sc - 1e-12: best...
          # accept current if sc_try < current_sc + ann*(best_sc)

          else: no_improve +=1; ann*=0.8; if no_improve>10: perturb a; x_warm=None

    return best

Pseudo:
```
def run_lbfgs_softmax(a0, beta_list, maxfun_per_beta=1000, maxiter=None, deadline=None):
    z = np.log(a0 + 1e-12)
    z -= z.mean()
    best_a=a0; best_sc=score
    for beta in beta_list:
       if deadline and time>deadline break
       maxfun = max(100, min(maxfun_per_beta, int((deadline-time)/ (0.02*max(1,n/1000)))))? 
       obj = SoftmaxLSEObj(beta)
       res = minimize(obj, z, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxiter':maxfun*2,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':20})
       a=softmax(res.x); sc=score; update; z=res.x - mean
    return best
```
Need avoid nested `obj` class with beta? We can instantiate `SoftmaxLSEObj(beta)`.

Class:
```
class SoftmaxLSEObj:
    def __init__(self, beta, reg=1e-9):
        self.beta = float(beta); self.reg=reg
    def __call__(self, z):
        val, grad = softmax_lse_and_grad(z, self.beta, self.reg)
        return val, grad
```
This is okay.

But `softmax_lse_and_grad` uses global constant? It needs n from z length. It calls `fft_conv_self(a)` and `trans_conv(a,p)`. Could optimize by precomputing? In each eval, a changes; need conv.

`trans_conv` with no cache: For z eval, a length n, p length m. It computes M3, Af=rfft(a), Pf=rfft(p[::-1]), h. That's two forwards + inverse. `fft_conv_self` computes one forward + inverse? Actually A=ffr(a), irfft(A*A). So total 3 forward/inverse per eval. L-BFGS eval heavy. Could combine by using same A? For self conv and trans conv, both need rfft(a). We can compute in `softmax_lse_and_grad` directly with FFT to share A. Let's implement a specialized `lse_score_grad_softmax(z, beta)` doing:
- a=softmax
- M2=next_pow2(2n-1); A=ffr(a,M2); c=ifft(A*A)[:M2?]
- For grad: M3 same? We can use M3=next_pow2(3n-2); A3=ffr(a,M3) (different M). Could maybe use M3 for self: A3, c=ifft(A3*A3)[:2n-1], gradient=ifft(A3 * rfft(p_rev,M3)). This uses one A forward? Actually A3 computed once; c uses A3*A3, grad uses A3*P. Total 1 forward (A) + 1 forward (p_rev) + 2 inverse per eval. Good. Use M3 large for all. For n=7000 M3=32768, okay. For exact score with M2 smaller, but M3 is fine. Implement inside `softmax_lse_and_grad` to avoid separate calls.

```
def softmax_lse_and_grad(z,beta,reg=1e-9):
   n=len(z)
   a = softmax(z)
   m=2*n-1
   M=next_pow2(3*n-2)
   A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
   c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, M)[:m]
   # p softmax beta*c
   zc=beta*c; zmax=...; exp; sumexp
   LSE = zmax + log(sumexp)/beta
   score = 2*n*LSE + reg*0.5*np.sum(z*z)? (but if we include reg, z in objective; gradient)
   p=exp/sumexp
   # gradient
   P = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], M)
   h = np.fft.irfft(A*P, M)[:3*n-2]
   g = h[m-1:m-n:-1].copy()
   d = 2*g
   mean_d = float(np.dot(a,d))
   grad = a*(d - mean_d) + reg*z
   return score, grad
```
Reg term: if val includes `0.5*reg*sum z²`, grad += reg*z. Good. But note `z` in softmax is invariant to constant; reg breaks.

`exact_score` should use smaller M for speed: `M=next_pow2(2n-1); A=rfft; c=irfft(A*A)[:2n-1]`.

LP solve can also optimize with shared A3.
In each iteration:
- X=ffr(x,M3?) Need c=a_lp*x. We can use M3 as well: Xf=ffr(x,M3); c=irfft(Af3*Xf,M3)[:m]. For gradient, Pf=ffr(p[::-1],M3); h=irfft(Af3*Pf,M3)[:3n-2]. So one forward for x and one for p + two inverse. Good. Use M3 always. For n=7000 M3=32768. For small n okay.

Function `lp_solve_correct` optimized:
```
def lp_solve_correct(a, M, beta, deadline, x0=None, lam=1e-3, x_max=1000.0):
   n=len(a); m=2*n-1; M2=3*n-2; M3=next_pow2(M2)
   # floor a
   a_lp = (1-floor)*a + floor*(1/n)
   a_lp/=a_lp.sum()
   Af = rfft(a_lp,M3)
   x = ...
   # if x0 not None, clip positive normalize? LP variable not normalized; if warm, use it
   phi_prev=None; best_phi=inf; best_x=x
   tau=0.9/(beta + lam)
   t=1; u=x.copy()
   count=0
   while time < deadline:
      Xf = rfft(x,M3)
      c = irfft(Af*Xf,M3)[:m]
      z=beta*(c-M); umax=z.max(); expz=...
      lse = umax + log(expz.sum())/beta
      phi = -x.sum() + 0.5*lam*dot(x,x) + lse
      # track
      if phi < best_phi - 1e-12:
         best_phi=phi; best_x=x.copy()
      # gradient
      P = rfft(p[::-1], M3)
      h = irfft(Af*P, M3)[:M2]
      g = h[m-1:m-n:-1]
      grad = g - 1.0 + lam*x
      # FISTA
      v = u - tau*grad
      v = np.clip(v, 0.0, x_max)
      t_new=(1+sqrt(1+4*t*t))/2
      u_new = v + (t-1)/t_new*(v-x)
      u_new=np.clip(u_new,0,x_max)
      # restart if phi increased too much from best? simple
      x = v; u = u_new; t=t_new
      if phi > best_phi + 0.05*(1+best_phi):
          t=1; u=x.copy(); tau*=0.5; if tau<1e-4: tau=1e-4
      count+=1
      if count%100==0 and time... check break
   return best_x
```
But FISTA with x update from u may jump; best_x tracked. We warm start next LP with best_x (not normalized? best_x is LP variable, can be >1). If x values up to x_max, okay. But if x has sum maybe large; next LP constraints independent. Good.

Need ensure `M` positive. We can compute `M = max(self_conv(a_norm).max())`. If a normalized sum1. If M very small? no.

`project_simplex(v)`: standard with sum target 1. Implementation robust:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    n=len(v); v=np.asarray(v,float)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(v)
    rho=np.nonzero(v*np.arange(1,n+1) > cssv - 1)[0]
    if len(rho)==0: return np.ones(n)/n
    r=rho[-1]
    theta=(v[:r+1].sum() - 1.0)/(r+1)
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
This assumes v maybe any scale, projects to sum1 nonnegative. If all huge? ok.

`fft_conv_self(a)` use array 1D float64.

if s<eps: return ones/n
return e/s
```

`gradient_refine(a, deadline)`:

def refine_sequence(a, deadline, rng=None):
    a=normalize(a)
    best_sc=score_exact(a)
    if best_sc < target? print

    # Early LBFGS

    if n < 4096:
       t_lbfgs = time + min(0.25*rem, 60)
       best = run_lbfgs_softmax(a, t_lbfgs, maxfun_per_beta=..., targets=[2,8,30])
    # LP
    t_lp = time + min(0.70*(deadline-time), ...)
    best = lp_alternate_correct(best, t_lp, rng)
    # Final LBFGS

    if rem>30:
       r2=min(0.5*rem, 120)

    # gradient refine small
    rem=deadline-time
    if rem>10: gradient_refine(best, min(deadline,time+0.2*rem))
    return best

Now `lp_alternate_correct` detailed code to avoid infinite loops:
```
def lp_alternate_correct(a, deadline, rng=None):
    a=normalize(a); n=len(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score_exact(a)
    current_score=best_sc
    x_warm=None
    no_improve=0
    solve_time = 1.0 if n>4096 else 2.0
    max_solve_time = 6.0 if n<2048 else 3.0
    beta_cycle = [100, 300, 800, 2000]
    outer=0
    while time < deadline - 2:
        if no_improve > 25:
            # diversify current if far from best: mix best with random init or uniform
            if current_score > best_sc + 1e-5:
                r= np.random... maybe use rng
                # random smooth small
                a = normalize(0.7*best_a + 0.3*random_smooth(n,rng))
                current_score=score_exact(a)
                x_warm=None
            no_improve=0
        for beta in beta_cycle:
            if time > deadline - 3: break
            # estimate M
            M=current_score/(2.0*n)
            if M <=0 or not finite: M=1e-8
            dl=min(deadline-1, time+solve_time)
            x_lp = lp_solve_correct(a, M, beta, dl, x0=x_warm)
            x_warm = x_lp.copy()
            Sx=x_lp.sum()
            if Sx <=1e-12: break
            x=x_lp/Sx
            a_new, sc_new = mix_line_search(a, x, current_score, deadline)
            if sc_new < best_sc:
                best_a=a_new; best_sc=sc_new
                # move current to best maybe
                a=a_new.copy(); current_score=sc_new; no_improve=0
                solve_time = min(max_solve_time, solve_time*1.15)
            else:
                # accept if small improvement relative current?
                if sc_new <= current_score + 0.001:
                    a=a_new.copy(); current_score=sc_new
                else:
                    # try t=0.1
                    a_small=normalize((1-0.1)*a + 0.1*x)
                    sc_small=score_exact(a_small) if time < deadline-0.5 else inf
                    if sc_small <= current_score + 0.005:
                       a=a_small; current_score=sc_small
                    no_improve +=1
                if no_improve % 5 ==0:
                    solve_time = max(0.3, min(max_solve_time, solve_time*1.2))
                if no_improve >= 30:
                    # reset and next beta/outer
                    x_warm=None
                    break
            if time > deadline - 3: break
    return best_a
```

Detailed function implementations:

`next_pow2(m)`:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(np.ceil(np.log2(m))))
```
Using np.log2 each time; can use bit_length:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```
This gives next power of two >=m. For m=16384, (16383).bit_length=14? 16383 binary 14 bits, 1<<14=16384. Good. For m=16385 -> 15 bits ->32768.

`fft_conv_self(a)`:
```
def fft_conv_self(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    m=2*n-1
    M=next_pow2(m)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,M)[:m]
    # remove tiny imaginary? irfft real
    return c
```
Potential numerical negative small; score uses max.

`score_exact(a)`:
```
def score_exact(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    S=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-12: return np.inf
    c=fft_conv_self(a)
    mx=float(c.max())
    if mx < 0: mx=0
    return float(2.0*n*mx/(S*S))
```

`normalize(a)` as above.

`resample_seq(a,n)`: if n<=1? no.
```
def resample_seq(a,n):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   if a.size==0: return np.ones(n)/n
   if a.size==n: a=a.copy()
   else:
     x_old=np.linspace(0,1,a.size); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
     a=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
   return normalize(a)
```

`project_simplex(v)` as above. Need if v all -inf? no.

`trans_conv(a,p)`:
```
def trans_conv(a,p):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); p=np.asarray(p,float)
   n=a.size; m=2*n-1; L=3*n-2; M=next_pow2(L)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
   P=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],M)
   h=np.fft.irfft(A*P,M)[:L]
   g=h[m-1:m-n:-1].copy()
   return g
```
For m-n? m=2n-1, m-n = n-1. Slice `h[2n-2 : n-1 : -1]` includes indices 2n-2,2n-3,...,n-1 length n. Good.

`softmax_fetch(z)` as above.

`softmax_lse_and_grad(z,beta,reg=1e-10)`:
```
def softmax_lse_and_grad(z,beta,reg=1e-10):
   z=np.asarray(z,float).ravel()
   n=z.size
   a=softmax_fetch(z)
   m=2*n-1; L=3*n-2; M=next_pow2(L)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,M)[:m]
   cmax=float(c.max())
   zc=beta*(c-cmax) # to make LSE relative? But if we subtract cmax, LSE = cmax + logsum/beta? For logsumexp(beta c) = beta cmax + logsum. Then LSE = cmax + logsum/beta. Good.
   zc_max=float(zc.max())? zc has max 0. We can do exp_z=np.exp(zc) because max 0? c-cmax <=0, max 0. exp_z[where max]=1. sum.
   sumexp=float(np.sum(np.exp(zc)))
   if sumexp<=0: sumexp=1
   lse=cmax + math.log(sumexp)/beta
   score=2.0*n*lse + 0.5*reg*float(np.dot(z,z))
   exp_z=np.exp(zc)
   p=exp_z/sumexp
   P=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], M)
   h=np.fft.irfft(A*P, M)[:L]
   g=h[m-1:m-n:-1].copy()
   d=2.0*g
   mean_d=float(np.dot(a,d))
   grad = a*(d-mean_d) + reg*z
   return score, grad
```
Subtracting cmax inside LSE: The objective LSE = cmax + logsum(beta(c-cmax))/beta. This is equivalent. For gradient of LSE wrt c is p where p=softmax(beta c) = softmax(beta(c-cmax)), derivative same. Good. This improves numeric and score close to cmax when beta large.

`SoftmaxLSEObj` class:
```
class SoftmaxLSEObj:
    def __init__(self, beta, reg=1e-10):
        self.beta=float(beta); self.reg=float(reg)
    def __call__(self, z):
        val, grad=softmax_lse_and_grad(z,self.beta,self.reg)
        return val, grad
```

`run_lbfgs_softmax`:
```
def run_lbfgs_softmax(a, deadline, maxfun_per_beta=2000, target_mults=(0.2,0.6,2.0)):
    a=normalize(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score_exact(a)
    n=len(a)
    rem=deadline-time
    if rem < 5: return best_a
    maxc=best_sc/(2*n)
    if maxc<=0: maxc=1.0/n
    base=15.0/maxc
    base=max(200.0, min(20000.0, base))
    betas=[]
    for mult in target_mults:
       b=base*mult
       if b<50: b=50
       if b>100000: b=100000
       if not any(abs(b-b0)<0.01*b0 for b0 in betas): betas.append(b)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-14)); z-=z.mean()
    M=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    t_eval_est=max(0.001, 2.2e-7*M + 0.002)
    for beta in betas:
       if time > deadline-3: break
       rem=deadline-time
       maxfun=int(min(maxfun_per_beta, max(50, rem/(t_eval_est*1.5))))
       if maxfun<20: continue
       maxiter=max(50, max(1, int(maxfun//3)))
       obj=SoftmaxLSEObj(beta, 1e-10)
       try:
          res=minimize(obj, z, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':20})
       except Exception:
          break
       a_new=softmax_fetch(res.x)
       sc=score_exact(a_new)
       if sc < best_sc:
          best_a=a_new.copy(); best_sc=sc
          z=res.x; z-=z.mean()
          # print optional
    return best_a
```
Note: `minimize` may call objective many times; if an exception due memory, break.

`lp_solve_correct`: Need import math for sqrt? Use `math.sqrt`.
```
def lp_solve_correct(a, M, beta, deadline, x0=None, floor=1e-6, lam=1e-3, x_max=1000.0):
    a=normalize(a)
    n=len(a); m=2*n-1; L=3*n-2; Mf=next_pow2(L)
    if floor>0:
       a_lp=(1.0-floor)*a + floor*(1.0/n)
       a_lp=a_lp/a_lp.sum()
    else: a_lp=a.copy()
    Af=np.fft.rfft(a_lp,Mf)
    if x0 is not None:
       x=np.asarray(x0,float).copy(); x=np.clip(x,0,x_max)
       if x.sum()<1e-12: x=np.ones(n)/n
    else:
       x=np.ones(n)/n
    u=x.copy(); t_mom=1.0
    # initial tau: if beta maybe 0? beta=max(1,beta)
    beta=float(max(1.0,beta))
    Lbound=beta + lam + 1e-12
    tau=0.8/Lbound
    best_phi=np.inf; best_x=x.copy()
    count=0
    while time.monotonic() + 0.02 < deadline:
       Xf=np.fft.rfft(x,Mf)
       c=np.fft.irfft(Af*Xf,Mf)[:m]

```
We have parameter `M` but also FFT size. Rename `Mval` in function signature? Use `target_m`. Function `lp_solve_correct(a, target_m, beta, deadline...)`.
   `z=beta*(c-target_m)`.
   zmax = z.max() (should ≤0 if feasible but maybe >0)
   `exp_z=np.exp(z-zmax); sumexp=...`
   lse=zmax + log(sumexp)/beta
   phi=-x.sum()+0.5*lam*dot(x,x)+lse
   if phi<best_phi: best_x=x.copy(); best_phi=phi
   p=exp_z/sumexp
   P=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],Mf)

   v=np.clip(v,0.0,x_max)

       # restart momentum, reduce step
       t_mom=1.0; u=x.copy(); tau=max(1e-5, tau*0.5)
   else:
       t_mom=t_new

       # If no phi improvement for many, maybe reduce tau? But best_phi tracked. Keep.
       pass

   # optional if abs(x - best_x) small and phi stable break? not.
```
Return best_x. The objective may have local? convex; best_x final. Good.

Check gradient sign: We minimize φ = -sum + lam/2 x² + LSE(β(Ax-M)). Derivative LSE = A^T p = g. derivative -sum = -1, penalty lam x. So grad = g -1 + lamx. FISTA x = proj(u - τ grad). If g=0, grad=-1, x increases. Good.

But if M target is small, as x grows c exceeds M, LSE gradient g positive, may reduce. Fine.

`mix_line_search` implementation with time:
```
def mix_line_search(a,b,current_score,deadline):
   a=normalize(a); b=normalize(b)
   best_sc=current_score; best_v=a.copy(); best_t=0.0
   ts=[0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,0.85,0.95,1.0]
   for t in ts:
      if time > deadline - 0.05: break
      v=(1-t)*a + t*b; v=normalize(v); sc=score_exact(v)
      if sc < best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_v=v.copy(); best_t=t
   if 0<best_t<1 and time < deadline-0.5:
       center=best_t; step=0.05
       candidates=[max(0.05,center-0.1), max(0.05,center-0.05), min(0.95,center+0.05), min(0.95,center+0.1), 0.5, 1.0]
       for t in candidates:
          if time > deadline - 0.05: break
          v=normalize((1-t)*a + t*b); sc=score_exact(v)
          if sc<best_sc: update
   return best_v, best_sc, best_t
```
This does ~13 scores. Could be many per LP. But LP solve dominates. If time too short, skip refine.

`lp_alternate_correct` with more time. Need return best score too? Return best_a only; global best can evaluate. But refine_sequence needs best. We can return best_a.

```
def lp_alternate_correct(a, deadline, rng=None):
    a=normalize(a); n=len(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score_exact(a)
    current=a.copy(); current_score=best_sc
    x_warm=None; no_improve=0
    if n>4096: solve_time=0.8; max_solve=4.0
    elif n>1024: solve_time=1.2; max_solve=5.0
    else: solve_time=2.0; max_solve=8.0
    betas=[100,300,800,2000]
    outer=0
    while time < deadline-2:
       if no_improve > 20:
           # perturb current if stuck: mix with best and a random smooth
           if rng is not None and best_sc < current_score+1e-8:
              r= make_random_smooth(n, rng)
              current=normalize(0.8*best_a + 0.2*r)
              current_score=score_exact(current)
           else:
              current=best_a.copy(); current_score=best_sc
           x_warm=None; no_improve=0
       for beta in betas:
          if time > deadline-3: break
          target_m = current_score/(2.0*n)
          if target_m <= 0 or not finite: target_m=1e-8
          dl=min(deadline-1, time+solve_time)
          x_lp=lp_solve_correct(current, target_m, beta, dl, x0=x_warm)
          x_warm=x_lp.copy()
          Sx=float(np.sum(x_lp))
          if Sx <=1e-12:
             continue
          x=x_lp/Sx
          a_line, sc_line, t_best = mix_line_search(current, x, current_score, deadline)
          if sc_line < best_sc - 1e-12:
             best_a=a_line.copy(); best_sc=sc_line
             # set current to best to continue from good point
             current=a_line.copy(); current_score=best_sc
             no_improve=0
             solve_time=min(max_solve, solve_time*1.2)
          else:
             # if line found a small improvement or same t=0, maybe accept if not t=0
             if t_best > 0.02 and sc_line <= current_score + 0.001:
                current=a_line.copy(); current_score=sc_line
                no_improve=0
             else:
                # if stuck, try small step t=0.1
                if time < deadline-0.5:
                   small=normalize(current + 0.1*(x-current))
                   sc_small=score_exact(small)
                   if sc_small < best_sc:
                       best_a=small.copy(); best_sc=sc_small; current=small.copy(); current_score=sc_small; no_improve=0
                   elif sc_small <= current_score + 0.01 and no_improve > 2:
                       current=small.copy(); current_score=sc_small; no_improve=0
                   else:
                       no_improve +=1
                else:
                   no_improve +=1
             if no_improve % 5 == 0:
                 solve_time = max(0.4, min(max_solve, solve_time*1.15))
             if no_improve >= 25:
                 break
       outer +=1
       if outer > 200: break
    return best_a
```
This might loop many. Need make_random_smooth top-level.

`make_random_smooth(n, rng)`:
```
def make_random_smooth(n,rng):
   if n<6: a=np.ones(n)
   else:
      noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
      k=max(3,n//30)
      kernel=np.ones(k)/k
      s=np.convolve(noise,kernel,'same')
      a=1.0 + 0.7*s
   return normalize(np.clip(a,0.01,1000))
```

`refine_sequence` should be top-level and use `run_lbfgs_softmax`, `lp_alternate_correct`. It needs `rng` for diversify. It returns array.
```
def refine_sequence(a, deadline, rng=None):
   a=normalize(a); n=len(a)
   best=score_exact(a)
   if time.monotonic() > deadline -2: return a
   # Early L-BFGS for small/medium
   rem=deadline-time
   if n <= 4096 and rem > 20:
      dl=time + min(0.20*rem, 120.0)
      a=run_lbfgs_softmax(a, dl, maxfun_per_beta=5000 if n<1024 else 2000)
      sc=scoreExact; update best
   # LP main
   rem=deadline-time
   rem_lp=0.62*rem if n>4096 else 0.50*rem
   dl=time + min(rem_lp, rem-5)
   a=lp_alternate_correct(a, dl, rng)
   sc=scoreExact; update
   # Final LBFGS
   rem=deadline-time
   if rem > 15:
      dl=time + min(0.35*rem, 180.0 if n>4096 else 60.0)
      a=run_lbfgs_softmax(a, dl, maxfun_per_beta=3000 if n<2048 else 1000)
      update
   # gradient refine
   rem=deadline-time
   if rem>10:
      dl=time + min(0.2*rem, 60.0)
      a=gradient_refine(a, dl, rng)
      update
   # Ensure best return (a may be worse after last? We should keep best separately inside and return best_a)
```

`gradient_refine` top-level:
```
def gradient_refine(a, deadline, rng=None):
   a=normalize(a); current=score_exact(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=current
   eta=1.0; no_improve=0
   for step in range(300):
      if time > deadline-1: break
      c=fft_conv_self(a); maxc=c.max()
      if maxc<=0: break
      beta=max(200.0, min(200000.0, 30.0/maxc))
      zc=beta*c; zc-=zc.max(); expz=np.exp(zc); p=expz/expz.sum()
      g=trans_conv(a,p)
      d=2.0*g; d=d-np.mean(d)
      maxd=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
      if maxd<1e-15: break
      base_eta=eta/(max(1.0, maxd*len(a)))
      best_step_sc=current; best_v=a
      for mult in (0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0):
         if time > deadline-0.2: break
         v=project_simplex(a - base_eta*mult*d)
         sc=score_exact(v)
         if sc<best_step_sc:
             best_step_sc=sc; best_v=v
      if best_step_sc < current - 1e-12:
         a=best_v; current=best_step_sc; no_improve=0
         if current < best_sc: best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=current
         eta=min(2.0, eta*1.05)
      else:
         no_improve +=1; eta = max(0.05, eta*0.7)
         if no_improve>15: break
   return best_a
```
This uses `project_simplex` sort each mult (4 sorts) could be heavy. Limit steps maybe 100 for large. Use if n>4096 reduce mult? Fine.

Potential import: `operator` not needed if using argscores.

Double-check `project_simplex` formula: It assumes sum target 1. If v has inf? no.
```
def project_simplex(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=v.size
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(v) # Wait should cumsum u, not v! Standard algorithm uses cumsum of sorted v. I wrote cssv=np.cumsum(v) incorrectly. Need cssv = np.cumsum(u).
    rho = np.nonzero(v? Actually condition uses (u - (k+1)*theta >0) equivalent rho = np.max(np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1,n+1) > cssv - 1)) using u not v.
```

```python
"""
Improved flat-polynomial optimizer for non-negative height sequences.

The objective is scale-invariant:
    score(a) = 2*n*max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2

This program uses a coarse-to-fine population search:
  1. Build diverse profiles at small lengths and refine them.
  2. Upsample the best profiles to larger lengths.
  3. Use a corrected convolution LP/FISTA solver as the main local driver.
  4. Polish with LSE-smoothed L-BFGS in softmax space and a few projected
     gradient steps.

The main algorithmic correction over the previous state of the art is the
gradient of the cross-convolution in the LP subproblem, which is now computed
correctly by FFT instead of by a pointwise sliding-window approximation.
"""

import time
import math

import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basic utilities
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    bad = ~np.isfinite(a)
    if np.any(bad):
        a = np.where(bad, 0.0, a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-14:
        return np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        n = 1
    if a.size == 0:
        return normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    if a.size == n:
        return normalize_sequence(a)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_sequence(b)


def add_variation_inits(inits, a):
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    if a.size and np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        inits.append(a)


def make_random_smooth(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 4:
        return normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
    k = max(3, n // 40)
    kernel = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / float(k)
    smooth = np.convolve(noise, kernel, mode="same")
    smooth = smooth - smooth.mean()
    a = 1.0 + 0.55 * smooth
    return normalize_sequence(np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0))


def build_initializations(n, rng, parent=None):
    n = int(n)
    inits = []

    if parent is not None and len(parent) > 0:
        add_variation_inits(inits, parent)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)

    # Basic shapes.
    add_variation_inits(inits, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    add_variation_inits(inits, i + 1.0)
    add_variation_inits(inits, (n - i) + 1.0)
    add_variation_inits(inits, 1.0 - np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0))
    add_variation_inits(inits, (1.0 - x + 1e-8) ** 1.0)
    add_variation_inits(inits, (x + 1e-8) ** 1.0)

    # Low-order trigonometric perturbations.
    for k in range(1, 7):
        add_variation_inits(inits, 1.0 + 0.3 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x))
        add_variation_inits(inits, 1.0 + 0.3 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * x))
    add_variation_inits(inits, 1.0 + 0.25 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x) + 0.15 * np.sin(6.0 * np.pi * x))
    add_variation_inits(inits, 1.0 + 0.25 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x) - 0.15 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x))

    # Power-law and beta-like shapes.
    for a in (0.15, 0.35, 0.6, 1.0, 1.6, 2.4):
        add_variation_inits(inits, (x + 1e-8) ** a)
        add_variation_inits(inits, (1.0 - x + 1e-8) ** a)

    for a, b in (
        (0.0, 0.0),
        (0.2, 1.0),
        (0.4, 1.4),
        (0.6, 0.6),
        (0.6, 1.4),
        (1.0, 0.4),
        (1.4, 0.4),
        (0.2, 0.15),
        (0.9, 0.9),
    ):
        add_variation_inits(inits, (x + 1e-7) ** a * (1.0 - x + 1e-7) ** b)

    # Exponentials and two humps.
    for lam in (3.0, 7.0, 13.0):
        add_variation_inits(inits, np.exp(lam * x))
        add_variation_inits(inits, np.exp(-lam * x))
        add_variation_inits(inits, np.exp(lam * x) + 0.4 * np.exp(-lam * x))

    for mu in (0.18, 0.34, 0.5, 0.66, 0.82):
        sig = 0.10 + 0.03 * abs(mu - 0.5)
        add_variation_inits(inits, 0.05 + np.exp(-((x - mu) / sig) ** 2))
        add_variation_inits(inits, 0.03 + np.exp(-((x - mu) / sig) ** 2) + 0.25 * np.exp(-((x - (1.0 - mu)) / sig) ** 2))

    # Smooth random profiles.
    add_variation_inits(inits, make_random_smooth(n, rng))
    add_variation_inits(inits, make_random_smooth(n, rng))
    add_variation_inits(inits, make_random_smooth(n, rng))

    # Keep the list reasonably small.
    return inits[:64]


def exact_convolution_self(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    mfft = next_pow2(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, mfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, mfft)[:m]
    return c


def exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    c = exact_convolution_self(a)
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.inf
    if mx < 0.0:
        mx = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))


def project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64) > cssv - 1.0)[0]
    if rho.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    r = int(rho[-1])
    theta = (float(np.sum(u[:r + 1])) - 1.0) / float(r + 1)
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 1e-14:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return out / s


def trans_convolution(a, p):
    """
    Returns g of length n where
        g_i = sum_l a_{l-i} p_l
    with valid indices.
    This is the transpose action of the Toeplitz convolution matrix C(a) on p.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if p.size != m:
        raise ValueError("p must have length 2*n-1")
    L = 3 * n - 2
    mfft = next_pow2(L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, mfft)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], mfft)
    h = np.fft.irfft(A * P, mfft)[:L]
    return h[m - 1:m - n:-1].copy()


def softmax_fetch(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return z.copy()
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        e = np.exp(z)
    e = np.where(np.isfinite(e), e, 0.0)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(z, dtype=np.float64) / float(z.size)
    return e / s


def softmax_lse_and_grad(z, beta, reg=1e-10):
    """
    LSE-smoothed score in the softmax simplex.
    Objective is 2n * LSE(conv(a,a), beta) plus a tiny ridge in z.
    """
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = z.size
    if n <= 0:
        return 1e100, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(z, dtype=np.float64)

    a = softmax_fetch(z)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    L = 3 * n - 2
    mfft = next_pow2(L)

    A = np.fft.rfft(a, mfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, mfft)[:m]
    cmax = float(np.max(c))

    with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        zz = beta * (c - cmax)
        ez = np.exp(zz)
    ez = np.where(np.isfinite(ez), ez, 0.0)
    sumexp = float(ez.sum())
    if sumexp <= 0.0:
        sumexp = 1.0
        ez = np.zeros_like(zz)
        ez[np.argmax(zz)] = 1.0
    lse = cmax + math.log(sumexp) / float(beta)
    val = 2.0 * n * lse + 0.5 * reg * float(np.dot(z, z))
    p = ez / sumexp

    P = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], mfft)
    h = np.fft.irfft(A * P, mfft)[:L]
    g = h[m - 1:m - n:-1].copy()

    d = 2.0 * g
    mean_d = float(np.dot(a, d))
    grad = a * (d - mean_d) + reg * z
    return float(val), grad


class SoftmaxLSEObjective:
    def __init__(self, beta, reg=1e-10):
        self.beta = float(beta)
        self.reg = float(reg)

    def __call__(self, z):
        val, grad = softmax_lse_and_grad(z, self.beta, self.reg)
        return val, grad


def sort_pairs_by_score(pairs):
    if not pairs:
        return []
    arrs = [a for _, a in pairs]
    scores = np.array([s for s, _ in pairs], dtype=np.float64)
    order = np.argsort(scores)
    return [(float(scores[idx]), arrs[idx]) for idx in order]


def choose_lp_betas(n, target_m):
    """
    A small beta schedule for LP subproblems.
    Larger beta enforces the linear constraints more sharply.
    """
    base = max(50.0, 12.0 / max(1e-18, float(target_m)))
    if n <= 1024:
        base = min(base, 12000.0)
    elif n <= 4096:
        base = min(base, 8000.0)
    else:
        base = min(base, 5000.0)
    base = max(base, 80.0)
    mults = (0.25, 0.6, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0)
    out = []
    for mu in mults:
        b = max(20.0, base * mu)
        if not out or abs(b - out[-1]) > 0.02 * out[-1]:
            out.append(float(b))
    return out[:4]


def lp_solve_fista(a, target_m, beta, deadline, x0=None, floor=1e-6, lam=1e-3, x_max=1000.0):
    """
    Solve the regularized LP surrogate

      minimize  -sum(x) + 0.5*lam*||x||^2
                + (1/beta)*logsumexp(beta*(conv(a,x)-target_m))
      subject to x >= 0.

    The quadratic term makes the problem bounded and strongly convex.  It is
    kept small, so the solution is close to the original LP direction.
    """
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    L = 3 * n - 2
    mfft = next_pow2(L)

    if floor > 0.0:
        a_lp = (1.0 - floor) * a + floor * (1.0 / float(n))
        a_lp = a_lp / float(a_lp.sum())
    else:
        a_lp = a.astype(np.float64, copy=True)

    Af = np.fft.rfft(a_lp, mfft)

    if x0 is not None:
        x = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, float(x_max))
        if float(x.sum()) < 1e-14:
            x = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        x = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    beta = max(1.0, float(beta))
    lam = max(1e-8, float(lam))
    x_max = float(x_max)

    u = x.copy()
    t_mom = 1.0
    tau = 0.7 / (beta + lam + 1e-12)

    best_x = x.copy()
    best_phi = np.inf
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() + 0.02 < deadline:
        Xf = np.fft.rfft(x, mfft)
        c = np.fft.irfft(Af * Xf, mfft)[:m]

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
            break

        z = beta * (c - float(target_m))
        zmax = float(np.max(z))
        with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
        ez = np.where(np.isfinite(ez), ez, 0.0)
        sumexp = float(ez.sum())
        if sumexp <= 0.0:
            sumexp = 1.0
            ez = np.zeros_like(z)
            ez[np.argmax(z)] = 1.0

        lse = zmax + math.log(sumexp) / beta
        phi = -float(np.sum(x)) + 0.5 * lam * float(np.dot(x, x)) + lse
        if not np.isfinite(phi):
            break

        if phi < best_phi:
            best_phi = phi
            best_x = x.copy()

        p = ez / sumexp
        P = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], mfft)
        h = np.fft.irfft(Af * P, mfft)[:L]
        g = h[m - 1:m - n:-1]

        grad = g - 1.0 + lam * x

        v = u - tau * grad
        v = np.clip(v, 0.0, x_max)

        t_new = 0.5 * (1.0 + math.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t_mom * t_mom))
        u_new = v + ((t_mom - 1.0) / t_new) * (v - x)
        u_new = np.clip(u_new, 0.0, x_max)

        # Restart if the surrogate appears to be climbing.
        if phi > best_phi + 0.05 * (1.0 + abs(best_phi)):
            t_mom = 1.0
            u = x.copy()
            tau = max(1e-6, 0.5 * tau)
        else:
            t_mom = t_new

        x = v
        u = u_new
        it += 1

        if it % 64 == 0 and x.max() > x_max * 0.999:
            # If we hit the cap, make future solves a little more conservative.
            tau = max(1e-7, 0.7 * tau)

    return np.asarray(best_x, dtype=np.float64)


def mix_line_search(a, b, current_score, deadline):
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    b = normalize_sequence(b)

    best_sc = float(current_score)
    best_v = a.copy()
    best_t = 0.0

    coarse_ts = (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.50, 0.70, 0.85, 0.95, 1.00)
    for t in coarse_ts:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.05:
            break
        v = (1.0 - t) * a + t * b
        v = normalize_sequence(v)
        sc = exact_score(v)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_v = v.copy()
            best_t = float(t)

    if 0.0 < best_t < 1.0 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.35:
        candidates = (
            max(0.02, best_t - 0.08),
            max(0.02, best_t - 0.03),
            min(0.98, best_t + 0.03),
            min(0.98, best_t + 0.08),
            0.45,
            0.55,
        )
        seen = []
        for t in candidates:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.05:
                break
            if any(abs(t - s) < 0.01 for s in seen):
                continue
            seen.append(t)
            v = (1.0 - t) * a + t * b
            v = normalize_sequence(v)
            sc = exact_score(v)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc)
                best_v = v.copy()
                best_t = float(t)

    return best_v, float(best_sc), float(best_t)


def lp_alternating_refine(a, deadline, rng=None):
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = a.size

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a)

    current = a.copy()
    current_sc = float(best_sc)

    x_warm = None
    no_improve = 0

    if n > 4096:
        solve_time = 0.8
        max_solve_time = 4.0
    elif n > 1024:
        solve_time = 1.3
        max_solve_time = 5.5
    elif n > 256:
        solve_time = 2.0
        max_solve_time = 8.0
    else:
        solve_time = 3.5
        max_solve_time = 12.0

    outer = 0
    while time.monotonic() + 3.0 < deadline:
        if no_improve > 20:
            # Diversify: mix the current point with the best and a random profile.
            if rng is not None and best_sc < current_sc + 1e-7:
                r = make_random_smooth(n, rng)
                current = normalize_sequence(0.75 * best_a + 0.25 * r)
                current_sc = exact_score(current)
            else:
                current = best_a.copy()
                current_sc = float(best_sc)
            x_warm = None
            no_improve = 0

        target_m = current_sc / (2.0 * n)
        if target_m <= 1e-18 or not np.isfinite(target_m):
            target_m = 1e-10

        for beta in choose_lp_betas(n, target_m):
            if time.monotonic() + 2.0 > deadline:
                break
            dl = min(deadline - 1.0, time.monotonic() + solve_time)
            x_lp = lp_solve_fista(current, target_m, beta, dl, x0=x_warm)
            x_warm = x_lp.copy()
            sx = float(np.sum(x_lp))
            if sx <= 1e-14:
                continue
            x_dir = x_lp / sx

            v, scv, t_best = mix_line_search(current, x_dir, current_sc, deadline)

            if scv < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_a = v.copy()
                best_sc = float(scv)
                current = best_a.copy()
                current_sc = float(best_sc)
                no_improve = 0
                solve_time = min(max_solve_time, 1.15 * solve_time)
            else:
                accepted = False
                if t_best > 0.03 and scv <= current_sc + 1e-7:
                    current = v.copy()
                    current_sc = float(scv)
                    no_improve = 0
                    accepted = True

                if not accepted and time.monotonic() + 1.0 < deadline:
                    # Small step towards the LP direction to avoid stalling.
                    small = normalize_sequence(current + 0.12 * (x_dir - current))
                    sc_small = exact_score(small)
                    if sc_small < best_sc - 1e-12:
                        best_a = small.copy()
                        best_sc = float(sc_small)
                        current = best_a.copy()
                        current_sc = float(best_sc)
                        no_improve = 0
                        accepted = True
                    elif sc_small <= current_sc + 1e-3 and no_improve > 2:
                        current = small.copy()
                        current_sc = float(sc_small)
                        no_improve = 0
                        accepted = True

                if not accepted:
                    no_improve += 1

                if no_improve % 4 == 0:
                    solve_time = max(0.35, min(max_solve_time, 1.18 * solve_time))

                if no_improve >= 24:
                    break

        outer += 1
        if outer > 400:
            break

    return best_a, best_sc


def run_lbfgs_softmax(a, deadline, maxfun_per_beta=3000, target_scale=14.0):
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a)
    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 3.0:
        return best_a

    maxc = best_sc / (2.0 * n)
    if maxc <= 1e-18:
        maxc = 1.0 / float(n)

    base = target_scale / maxc
    base = max(200.0, min(18000.0, base))

    betas = []
    for mu in (0.22, 0.6, 1.6, 3.8):
        b = max(50.0, min(80000.0, base * mu))
        if not betas or abs(b - betas[-1]) > 0.02 * betas[-1]:
            betas.append(float(b))
    betas = betas[:4]

    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-16))
    z -= float(np.mean(z))

    mfft = next_pow2(max(4, 3 * n - 2))
    t_eval_est = max(0.002, 2.4e-7 * mfft + 0.0015)

    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
            break

        remaining = max(1.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
        maxfun = int(min(maxfun_per_beta, max(60, remaining / (1.6 * t_eval_est))))
        if maxfun < 25:
            continue
        maxiter = max(40, max(1, maxfun // 3))

        obj = SoftmaxLSEObjective(beta, reg=1e-10)
        try:
            res = minimize(
                obj,
                z,
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "ftol": 1e-13,
                    "gtol": 1e-9,
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "maxls": 20,
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if not hasattr(res, "x") or not np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            break

        a_try = softmax_fetch(res.x)
        sc = exact_score(a_try)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_a = a_try.copy()
            best_sc = float(sc)
            z = res.x.copy()
            z -= float(np.mean(z))

    return best_a


def projected_lse_refine(a, deadline):
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a)

    eta = 0.9
    no_improve = 0

    for _ in range(240):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1.0:
            break

        c = exact_convolution_self(a)
        maxc = float(np.max(c))
        if maxc <= 1e-18:
            break

        beta = max(250.0, min(120000.0, 24.0 / maxc))
        z = beta * c
        z -= float(np.max(z))
        with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            ez = np.exp(z)
        ez = np.where(np.isfinite(ez), ez, 0.0)
        Sum = float(ez.sum())
        if Sum <= 0.0:
            break
        p = ez / Sum

        g = trans_convolution(a, p)
        d = 2.0 * g
        d = d - float(np.mean(d))
        maxd = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
        if maxd <= 1e-16:
            break

        base_step = eta / max(1.0, maxd * float(n))
        step_best_v = a
        step_best_sc = exact_score(a)

        for mu in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.25:
                break
            v = project_simplex(a - base_step * mu * d)
            sc = exact_score(v)
            if sc < step_best_sc:
                step_best_sc = float(sc)
                step_best_v = v.copy()

        if step_best_sc < exact_score(a) - 1e-12:
            a = step_best_v
            no_improve = 0
            eta = min(2.0, 1.04 * eta)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            eta = max(0.04, 0.65 * eta)
            if no_improve > 14:
                break

        if exact_score(a) < best_sc:
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_sc = float(exact_score(a))

    return best_a


def refine_candidate(a, deadline, rng=None):
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = a.size

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a)

    def accept(trial, label=None):
        nonlocal best_a, best_sc
        s = exact_score(trial)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_sc:
            best_a = np.asarray(trial, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            best_sc = float(s)
            if label is not None:
                print(f"      [{label}] improve to {best_sc:.9f}")

    # 1. Small local LSE polish, useful especially at small/medium lengths.
    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 15.0 and n <= 8192:
        dl = time.monotonic() + min(0.22 * rem, 120.0)
        a_try = run_lbfgs_softmax(
            a,
            dl,
            maxfun_per_beta=7000 if n <= 1024 else (3500 if n <= 4096 else 1500),
            target_scale=14.0,
        )
        accept(a_try, "stage LBFGS")

    a = best_a

    # 2. Main corrected LP alternating phase.
    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 12.0:
        rem_lp = 0.58 * rem if n > 4096 else 0.50 * rem
        dl = time.monotonic() + min(rem_lp, max(4.0, rem - 8.0))
        a_try, _ = lp_alternating_refine(a, dl, rng=rng)
        accept(a_try, "stage LP")
        a = best_a

    # 3. High-resolution LSE polish.
    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 10.0:
        dl = time.monotonic() + min(0.35 * rem, 180.0 if n <= 4096 else 320.0)
        a_try = run_lbfgs_softmax(
            a,
            dl,
            maxfun_per_beta=5000 if n <= 2048 else 2200,
            target_scale=26.0,
        )
        accept(a_try, "final LBFGS")

    # 4. A little aggressive projected-gradient peak reduction.
    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 8.0:
        dl = time.monotonic() + min(0.18 * rem, 80.0)
        a_try = projected_lse_refine(a, dl)
        accept(a_try, "grad peak")

    return best_a


def run_population_stage(pool, n, stage_deadline, rng, keep):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        n = 256

    if time.monotonic() >= stage_deadline:
        if pool:
            return [resample_sequence(a, n) for a in pool[:max(1, keep)]]
        return [normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)) / float(n)]

    candidates = []

    if pool:
        for a in pool[:keep + 1]:
            candidates.append(resample_sequence(a, n))

    fresh = build_initializations(n, rng, None)
    # More initialization diversity when the stage has time.
    init_budget = max(2, min(16, int((stage_deadline - time.monotonic()) / 1.5)))
    candidates.extend(fresh[:init_budget])

    scored = []
    for a in candidates:
        a = normalize_sequence(a)
        s = exact_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            scored.append((float(s), a))

    if not scored:
        scored = [(2.0, normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)) / float(n))]

    sorted_pairs = sort_pairs_by_score(scored)

    total = max(0.0, stage_deadline - time.monotonic())
    min_time = 2.0
    while len(sorted_pairs) > 1 and total / float(len(sorted_pairs)) < min_time:
        sorted_pairs = sorted_pairs[:-1]

    out = []
    if sorted_pairs:
        per = total / float(len(sorted_pairs))
        for sc0, a0 in sorted_pairs:
            if time.monotonic() + 1.0 >= stage_deadline:
                out.append((exact_score(a0), a0))
                continue
            dl = time.monotonic() + min(per * 1.15, stage_deadline - 0.5)
            a1 = refine_candidate(a0, min(dl, stage_deadline), rng=rng)
            sc1 = exact_score(a1)
            out.append((float(sc1), a1))

    out_sorted = sort_pairs_by_score(out)
    return [a for _, a in out_sorted[:max(1, keep)]]


def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400):
    """
    Search for a non-negative height sequence minimizing the evaluation score.

    The function returns a Python list of floats.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + budget - 25.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(20240607)

    parent = None
    try:
        p = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if p is not None:
            p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if p.size > 32:
                parent = normalize_sequence(p)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        global_best_a = parent.copy()
        main_len = int(parent.size)
    else:
        main_len = 7000
        global_best_a = normalize_sequence(np.ones(main_len, dtype=np.float64) / float(main_len))

    if main_len < 256:
        main_len = 512
    if main_len > 12000:
        main_len = min(main_len, 10000)

    global_best_sc = exact_score(global_best_a)
    print(f"Initial score (len={len(global_best_a)}): {global_best_sc:.9f}")

    total_useful = max(60.0, deadline - t0)

    # Coarse-to-fine population stages.  These are most useful for escaping
    # poor local basins before spending most of the budget at full length.
    coarse_lengths = []
    for nl in (256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096):
        if nl < main_len:
            coarse_lengths.append(nl)

    coarse_frac = 0.32
    coarse_deadline = t0 + coarse_frac * total_useful
    stage_fracs = [0.08, 0.12, 0.18, 0.26, 0.36]
    pool = []
    stage_time = t0

    if coarse_lengths:
        for idx, nl in enumerate(coarse_lengths):
            if stage_time + 3.0 >= coarse_deadline:
                break
            fr = stage_fracs[idx] if idx < len(stage_fracs) else stage_fracs[-1]
            dl = min(coarse_deadline, stage_time + fr * total_useful)
            keep = 6 if nl <= 512 else (5 if nl <= 2048 else 3)
            print(f"\nStage n={nl} deadline={dl - t0:.1f}s, keep={keep}")
            pool = run_population_stage(pool, nl, dl, rng, keep)
            stage_time = dl
            if pool:
                sc = exact_score(pool[0])
                if sc < global_best_sc:
                    global_best_a = pool[0].copy()
                    global_best_sc = float(sc)
                    print(f"  stage best {sc:.9f}")

    # If we skipped some coarse stage, use leftover coarse time for 4096 if
    # useful.
    if time.monotonic() + 20.0 < coarse_deadline and main_len > 4096:
        print("\nExtra coarse stage n=4096")
        pool = run_population_stage(pool, 4096, coarse_deadline, rng, 3)
        if pool:
            sc = exact_score(pool[0])
            if sc < global_best_sc:
                global_best_a = pool[0].copy()
                global_best_sc = float(sc)
                print(f"  extra coarse best {sc:.9f}")

    # Final length exploration.
    # Reserve some time for an alternate length, often slightly larger, because
    # discretization can matter near the target value.
    if main_len == 8192:
        alt_len = 10000
    elif main_len < 8192:
        alt_len = 8192
    else:
        alt_len = 10000 if main_len != 10000 else 8192

    use_alt = (alt_len != main_len and alt_len <= 12000 and 0.12 * total_useful > 90.0)
    main_final_deadline = t0 + (0.78 * total_useful if use_alt else 0.93 * total_useful)

    finalists = []
    if pool:
        for a in pool[:3]:
            finalists.append(resample_sequence(a, main_len))
    if parent is not None:
        finalists.append(resample_sequence(parent, main_len))
    else:
        inits = build_initializations(main_len, rng, None)
        if inits:
            finalists.append(inits[0])
        if len(inits) > 5:
            finalists.append(inits[5])

    # Sort finalists by raw score and keep a manageable number.
    finals_scored = []
    for a in finalists:
        s = exact_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            finals_scored.append((float(s), a))
    finals_scored = sort_pairs_by_score(finals_scored)

    # If too many, cut down so each gets meaningful time.
    max_finals = 4
    while len(finals_scored) > max_finals and (main_final_deadline - time.monotonic()) / float(len(finals_scored)) < 30.0:
        finals_scored = finals_scored[:-1]

    print(f"\nMain final length={main_len}, finalists={len(finals_scored)}")
    if main_final_deadline - t0 > 20.0 and finals_scored:
        main_window = main_final_deadline - time.monotonic()
        per = main_window / float(len(finals_scored))
        for rank, (s0, a0) in enumerate(finals_scored):
            if time.monotonic() + 5.0 >= main_final_deadline:
                break
            dl = time.monotonic() + min(per * 1.12, main_final_deadline - 1.0)
            print(f"  final main candidate {rank}: start {s0:.9f}, budget {dl - time.monotonic():.1f}s")
            a1 = refine_candidate(a0, dl, rng=rng)
            s1 = exact_score(a1)
            if s1 < global_best_sc:
                global_best_a = a1.copy()
                global_best_sc = float(s1)
                print(f"    main final new best {s1:.9f}")

    # Alternate length polish, if we still have meaningful time.
    if use_alt and time.monotonic() + 80.0 < deadline:
        alt_deadline = deadline - 8.0
        alt_finalists = []
        alt_finalists.append(resample_sequence(global_best_a, alt_len))
        if parent is not None:
            alt_finalists.append(resample_sequence(parent, alt_len))
        inits_alt = build_initializations(alt_len, rng, None)
        if inits_alt:
            alt_finalists.append(inits_alt[0])

        alt_scored = []
        for a in alt_finalists:
            s = exact_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(s):
                alt_scored.append((float(s), a))
        alt_scored = sort_pairs_by_score(alt_scored)
        while len(alt_scored) > 1 and (alt_deadline - time.monotonic()) / float(len(alt_scored)) < 25.0:
            alt_scored = alt_scored[:-1]

        print(f"\nAlternate final length={alt_len}, finalists={len(alt_scored)}")
        if alt_scored:
            per = (alt_deadline - time.monotonic()) / float(len(alt_scored))
            for rank, (s0, a0) in enumerate(alt_scored):
                if time.monotonic() + 5.0 >= alt_deadline:
                    break
                dl = time.monotonic() + min(per * 1.10, alt_deadline - 1.0)
                print(f"  alt final candidate {rank}: start {s0:.9f}, budget {dl - time.monotonic():.1f}s")
                a1 = refine_candidate(a0, dl, rng=rng)
                s1 = exact_score(a1)
                if s1 < global_best_sc:
                    global_best_a = a1.copy()
                    global_best_sc = float(s1)
                    print(f"    alt final new best {s1:.9f}")

    # Last-ditch direct polish of the global best.
    if time.monotonic() + 25.0 < deadline:
        dl = deadline - 3.0
        a_try, _ = lp_alternating_refine(global_best_a, dl, rng=rng)
        sc = exact_score(a_try)
        if sc < global_best_sc:
            global_best_a = a_try.copy()
            global_best_sc = float(sc)
            print(f"Last LP polish: {sc:.9f}")

    out = np.asarray(global_best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(out.sum()) < 0.01 or not np.all(np.isfinite(out)):
        out = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0

    out_list = [float(x) for x in out]

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
        print("\nFinal internal score:", float(global_best_sc))
        print("Final verified score:", verified)
        print("Final length:", len(out_list))
        print("Elapsed seconds:", time.monotonic() - t0)
    except Exception:
        print("Internal score:", float(global_best_sc), "length:", len(out_list))

    return out_list
```
